post-17705 post type-post status-publish format-standard has-post-thumbnail hentry category-inside-cyanite\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_post_meta_wrapper\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<h1 class=\"entry-title\">Empowering researchers with free AI music analysis: Cyanite for innovators spotlight</h1>\n\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<p class=\"post-meta\"> by <span class=\"author vcard\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/blog/author/vincent/\" title=\"Posts by Vincent\" rel=\"author\">Vincent</a></span> | <span class=\"published\">Apr 14, 2025</span> | <a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/category/inside-cyanite/\" rel=\"category tag\">Inside Cyanite</a></p><img src=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/Spotlight_Magnified_Updated.png\" alt=\"\" class=\"\" width=\"1080\" height=\"675\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t<div class=\"entry-content\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<p class=\"last-updated\">Last updated on May 5th, 2026 at 12:41 pm</p><div class=\"et-l et-l--post\">\n\t\t\t<div class=\"et_builder_inner_content et_pb_gutters3\"><div class=\"et_pb_section et_pb_section_0 et_section_regular\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_row et_pb_row_0 et_pb_gutters1\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_0  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_0  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2><strong>We believe in the power of our AI music analysis tools to fuel creativity across diverse fields, from the arts and research to grassroots movements and creative coding. </strong></h2>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">That’s why we launched Cyanite for Innovators in <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2023/10/04/cyanite-for-innovators-launch/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">2023</a></span>, a support programme designed to empower individuals and teams working on non-commercial projects across the arts, research and creative coding communities. Participants gain access to our AI music analysis tools, opening the door for groundbreaking experimentation in areas like interactive art, digital installations, and AI-driven research. </span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">So far, we’ve received 20+ applications, five of which have been selected for the programme:</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_1  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h3>1. <a href=\"#1\"><span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\">Music and Dance Visualizer by Dilucious</span></a></h3>\n<h3>2. <a href=\"#2\"><span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\">Soundsketcher</span></a></h3>\n<h3>3. <a href=\"#3\"><span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\">MusiQ</span></a></h3>\n<h3>4. <a href=\"#4\"><span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\">Music and Dance Visualizer by Dilucious</span></a></h3>\n<h3>5. <a href=\"#5\"><span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\">Thesis: What is the greatest factor in making a timeless song?</span></a></h3></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_2  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><p>&nbsp;</p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Are you working on a groundbreaking AI-driven project? Apply now and join a community of innovators shaping the future.</span></p>\n<h2></h2>\n<h2><strong>How to Apply</strong></h2>\n<p><strong></strong></p>\n<p><strong>1.</strong> Click the button below.</p>\n<p><strong>2.</strong>&nbsp;Submit a detailed proposal outlining your project’s objectives, timeline, and expected outcomes.</p>\n<p><strong>3.</strong> Allow us 4-8 weeks to review your application and get back to you.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_button_module_wrapper et_pb_button_0_wrapper et_pb_button_alignment_center et_pb_module \">\n\t\t\t\t<a class=\"et_pb_button et_pb_button_0 et_pb_bg_layout_light\" href=\"https://form.typeform.com/to/tmwGiBmY\" target=\"_blank\" data-icon=\"5\">Apply Now</a>\n\t\t\t</div><div id=\"1\" class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_3  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2>Music and Dance Visualizer by Dilucious</h2>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><b><strong></strong></b></span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><b><strong><a href=\"https://dilucious.com/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">Agustin Di Luciano</span></a></strong></b>, a digital artist and developer, is pushing the limits of real-time interactivity with audio processing, motion capture, procedural generation, and Cyanite’s AI music analysis /ML technology. </span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">His project creates immersive, AI-powered sensory landscapes, transforming movement and sound into stunning real-time visuals. These images are from his recent exhibition at the Art Basel Miami where he showed his Music Visualizer to Miami’s top tech entrepreneurs as well as notable Latin American art collectors.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_image et_pb_image_0\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/image.png\" class=\"et_pb_lightbox_image\" title=\"\"><span class=\"et_pb_image_wrap \"><img decoding=\"async\" src=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/image.png\" alt=\"\" title=\"image\" class=\"wp-image-17717\"></span></a>\n\t\t\t</div><div id=\"2\" class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_4  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2>Soundsketcher</h2>\n<p>Led by <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><b><strong><a href=\"https://www.linkedin.com/in/asteris-zacharakis-phd-8359a917\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">Asteris Zacharakis</span></a></strong></b></span>, PhD and funded by the Hellenic Foundation for Research and Innovation, <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><b><strong><a href=\"https://soundsketcher.web.auth.gr/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">Soundsketcher </span></a></strong></b></span>(2024–2025) is a cutting-edge research project that blends computational, cognitive, and arts-based methodologies to develop an application that automatically converts audio into graphic scores.</p>\n<p>This technology assists users in learning, analyzing, and co-creating music, making music notation and composition more accessible and intuitive.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module ba_image_carousel href=\"https://www.linkedin.com/in/kristof-kasza/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">Kristóf Kasza </span></a></strong></b>developed MusiQ for his thesis at Budapest University of Technology and Economics, a multi-user service that sorts song requests based on parameters analyzed by Cyanite, using a FastAPI backend with Docker and a Flutter-based frontend. </span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Cyanite’s AI music analysis API enabled precise music sorting by key, genre, BPM, and mood, contributing to the project’s success, which earned him top marks, and while he has since started a full-time software development job, he looks forward to further enhancing MusiQ in the future.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module Insights</strong></h2>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">A research team arround <b><strong><a href=\"https://www.linkedin.com/in/j%C3%B6rg-m%C3%BChlhans-928a74aa\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">Dr. Jörg Mühlhans </span></a></strong></b>from the University of Vienna, Institute of Musicology is conducting a large-scale quantitative analysis of 125 queer music songs, revealing key trends in emotional tone and queer representation, and aims to integrate Cyanite’s AI to validate, expand, and refine these insights for future research.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div id=\"5\" class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_7  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2>Thesis: What is the greatest factor in making a timeless song?</h2>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><b><strong><a dissertation to investigate the key musical, historical/cultural, and economic factors that contribute to a “timeless song”—one that resonates across generations—aiming to determine the most influential elements and provide insights for musicians seeking to understand and apply these trends.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div id=\"1\" class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_8 This includes music publishers, production music libraries, sync and sound branding departments, music-tech startups, TV networks, and music distribution services. Moreover, Cyanite’s web app is accessible to artists and producers seeking efficient music tagging and marketing solutions. To learn more, head to our <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/use-cases/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">Use Cases</a></span> page.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_2  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">What are Cyanite’s key-features and benefits?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>We pride ourselves to be regarded as the best company for Auto-Tagging and Auto-Descriptions. Moreover, some of the biggest music companies on the globe use our powerful search capabilities such as Similarity Search &amp; Free Text Search. These algorithms not only enable faster and more accurate searches but ensure that the whole music catalog is leveraged instead of just a part of it.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_3  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h1 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Getting Started</h1>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_4  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">How can I use Cyanite’s music tagging and search solutions?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>You can leverage Cyanite’s music tagging and search solutions through our API, <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://app.cyanite.ai/library\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">Web App</a></span>, or integrations into the biggest <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2024/07/30/music_content_management_systems_with_cyanite/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">catalog management systems</a></span>. The Cyanite API is ideal for music businesses who want to tailor our capabilities to their product as part of their user experience. Meanwhile, the web app serves as an intuitive AI search and curation tool. Fast to set up and easy to use. For examples of Cyanite integrations take a look at our <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"http://www.cyanite.ai/#inspirations\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">inspirations</a></span> section.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_5  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">How can I get started?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>You can easily start using Cyanite by signing up for our <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"https://app.cyanite.ai/library\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">Web App</a></span> where you can upload 5 songs for free per month. For large catalog uploads, ingestion of multiple audio formats (incl. wav, mp4, or FLAC) is also possible.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_8  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">I have a large catalog of music. If you are a business with a larger catalog, please reach out via <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"mailto:business@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">business@cyanite.ai</a></span> to request our pricing calculator, factoring in your catalog size and the extent of our services. You can also fill out our request form <a href=\"https://form.typeform.com/to/ntzoApKn\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">here</span></a> and our team will get back to you as soon as possible.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_15  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Can I use Cyanite for my research or a non-commercial project?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>We support selected non-commercial projects through our Cyanite For Innovators program. You can learn more about this program and how to apply <a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2023/10/04/cyanite-for-innovators-launch/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">here</span></a>.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_16  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h1 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Support &amp; Troubleshooting</h1>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_17  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Can Cyanite’s AI results be exported?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Currently, the results from the library view cannot be exported for private users. If you are a business, you can request a CSV file with your tags and data from <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"mailto:business@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">business@cyanite.ai</a></span>. If you use the API, you will receive the detailed results as a JSON file as part of your plan.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_18  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Can Cyanite’s results be edited?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>No, currently the results cannot be edited within Cyanite. If you feel that a tag is wrong, please contact <a href=\"mailto:support@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">support@cyanite.ai</span></a> so we can look into the matter.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_19  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">A specific tag I need is missing. How can I add it?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>We can develop customized tags or even taxonomies for businesses. Reach out to <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"mailto:business@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">business@cyanite.ai</a></span> to find out more about custom tagging.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_20  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Is my data secure when I upload tracks to Cyanite?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Yes, all data stored on our servers are GDPR compliant. We offer copyright-safe uploads for businesses. To learn more, please read <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a target=\"_blank\" url=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/01/05/how-cyanite-protects-your-sensitive-audio-privacy-first-workflows-for-every-catalog/\" href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/01/05/how-cyanite-protects-your-sensitive-audio-privacy-first-workflows-for-every-catalog/\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">this article.</a></span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_21  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Why are my tags not as expected?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Cyanite technology has an accuracy rate of up to 99%. However, it can always happen that a tag does not meet expectations. We constantly strive to achieve the best-in-class results. If you are dissatisfied or need assistance, please contact <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"mailto:support@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">support@cyanite.ai</a></span>.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_22  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Why are my results in the Similarity Search not as expected?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Similarity Search results are only as good as the song library. We suggest using the Similarity Search on a catalog of at least 1,000 songs. Contact <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"mailto:support@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">support@cyanite.ai</a></span> if you need assistance.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_23  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\"> How does the Similarity Search differ from the Spotify algorithms?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Our Similarity Search is specifically built for B2B purposes. It is fit to find songs that sound most closely to a given input track. Spotify builds playlists for B2C purposes with songs that go in a similar artistic direction in terms of niche or subculture.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_24  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Does the Free Text Search also understand musical keywords?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Like any other prompt-based search, specificity is the key to success. The more details you provide, the better your results will be. Please check out our <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2023/11/24/how-to-prompt-using-cyanites-free-text-search-2/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">prompt-guide</a></span> to assist in writing great prompts for music.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_25  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">I only have a couple of tracks. How big must a catalog be to be ingested into Cyanite?</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>We have use cases literally from the first track on. If you only have a few tracks, you can easily buy our <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"https://app.cyanite.ai/profile#Subscription%20and%20billing\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">web app subscription</a></span> for 20 tracks per month or single analysis credits a la carte. For bigger libraries, please reach out to <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"mailto:business@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">business@cyanite.ai</a></span>.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_26  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">I have an idea but I’m not sure if it’s even technically possible.</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Just reach out to&nbsp;<span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a class=\"gtrackexternal\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"mailto:business@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">business@cyanite.ai</a></span>&nbsp;and we’ll talk about it.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_27  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h1 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Web App</h1>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_28  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">I want to delete my account.</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Please send us an email to <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" href=\"mailto:support@cyanite.ai\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">support@cyanite.ai</a></span> and we will take care of it. Give us a couple of days to process your requests and please note that this can not be undone.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_29  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h5 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">I want to cancel my subscription (for paying customers only).</h5>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>Sorry to hear that you will be leaving us. subscription.</p></div>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t</div>\t\t</div>\n\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t</article>\n\n\t\t\t\n\n</div>\n\n\t<footer class=\"entry-title\">Best AI tools for music tagging and search infrastructure (2026)</h1>\n\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<p class=\"post-meta\"> by <span class=\"author vcard\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/blog/author/jakob/\" title=\"Posts by Jakob\" rel=\"author\">Jakob</a></span> | <span class=\"published\">May 4, 2026</span> | <a 02:59 pm</p><div class=\"et-l et-l--post\">\n\t\t\t<div class=\"et_builder_inner_content et_pb_gutters3\"><div class=\"et_pb_section et_pb_section_0 et_section_regular\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_row et_pb_row_0\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_0  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_0  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><div data-test-render-count=\"1\">\n<div class=\"group\">\n<div class=\"contents\">\n<div class=\"group relative relative pb-3\" data-is-streaming=\"false\">\n<div class=\"font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8\">\n<div>\n<div class=\"standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown\">\n<p class=\"font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]\"><strong>See how Cyanite performs on your own catalog. <a href=\"https://app.cyanite.ai/register?utm_source=blog&amp;utm_medium=article&amp;utm_campaign=best-ai-music-tools-top\">Try it free for 14 days</a>.</strong></p>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n<div class=\"flex justify-start\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Message actions\">\n<div class=\"text-text-300\">\n<div class=\"text-text-300 flex items-stretch justify-between\">\n<div class=\"w-fit\" data-state=\"closed\">\n<div class=\"relative text-text-500 group-hover/btn:text-text-100\">\n<div class=\"absolute top-0 left-0 transition-all opacity-0 scale-50\">Many music catalogs are growing faster than manual workflows can handle. What used to be manageable with spreadsheets and human tagging now requires infrastructure that can make tens of thousands of tracks discoverable.</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">“AI tools for music” is one of the most searched terms in this space, but also one of the most misleading. Metadata generation, file management, distribution workflows, and search infrastructure each require different solutions. Choosing the wrong category is the most common reason implementations fail.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">This guide focuses specifically on AI tagging and search infrastructure, so you can evaluate your options based on how these systems actually work.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_1  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">What to look for in AI tagging and search infrastructure</span></h2>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Not all AI music tools are designed for the same purpose. Before evaluating specific tools, it helps to understand what separates infrastructure-grade systems from standalone tools.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_2  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Audio-based vs. metadata-based analysis</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Does the system analyze the audio signal directly, or rely on existing metadata and text inputs? Audio-based systems produce more consistent results, particularly for large or legacy catalogs where existing metadata is incomplete or unreliable.</span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_3  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><ul>\n<li><b>Tagging quality and consistency</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Consistency matters more than coverage. A system that tags reliably at scale is more operationally valuable than one that only produces detailed results for some tracks.</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_4  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><ul>\n<li><b>Search capabilities</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">:</span> <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Look for similarity search based on a reference song, the ability to use multiple tracks as a reference, and free-text search that understands natural language prompts rather than requiring exact keyword matches.</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_5  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><ul>\n<li><b>Control over discovery</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Good infrastructure lets you filter by tags or custom metadata, combine similarity search with business logic, and adjust ranking behavior based on your use case.</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_6  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><ul>\n<li><b>API flexibility and integration depth</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: A system that cannot integrate cleanly into your existing pipelines doesn’t function as infrastructure. It’s a standalone tool.</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_7  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><ul>\n<li><b>Catalog scale and processing performance</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Evaluate processing speed per track, batch handling capacity, and how smoothly the system onboards a large or legacy catalog.</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_8  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><ul>\n<li><b>Data security and ownership</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Consider the following questions: Is your audio processed locally or on third-party servers? Is your data used to train external AI models? How does the system handle unreleased content? Is it GDPR-compliant?</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_9  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">AI software for analyzing music at a glance</span></h2>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Before we dive into the leading AI tagging and search tools, here’s a quick feature overview.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_image et_pb_image_0\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.21.18@2x.png\" class=\"et_pb_lightbox_image\" title=\"\"><span class=\"et_pb_image_wrap \"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1576\" height=\"2512\" src=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.21.18@2x.png\" alt=\"\" title=\"AI software for analyzing music at a glance\" href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/02/19/everything-youve-ever-wanted-to-know-about-cyanite-answering-your-faqs/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><b>Everything you’ve ever wanted to know about Cyanite (answering your FAQs)</b></a></span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_11  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Tool-by-tool breakdown</span></h2>\n<h3><strong></strong></h3></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_12  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h3><strong>Cyanite</strong></h3>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Cyanite serves over 200 music companies and 200,000 artists worldwide. It’s the only tool in this group that </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/01/13/from-upload-to-output-how-cyanite-turns-audio-into-reliable-metadata-at-scale/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">combines auto-tagging and search in a single end-to-end workflow</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">, with audio that’s processed exclusively on EU-based servers and </span><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/01/05/how-cyanite-protects-your-sensitive-audio-privacy-first-workflows-for-every-catalog/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\">never used to train external AI models</span></span></a><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">.&nbsp;</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">How you can get started with Cyanite:</span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Via </span><b>API integration</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> into proprietary platforms</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Through the </span><b>Cyanite web app</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">, with no development required</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Via a </span><b>Tagging Export</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">, delivered as a CSV or Google Spreadsheet, for teams that need tagged metadata without building a full integration</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">We also </span><b>integrate natively with major catalog management systems</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">, including:</span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">SourceAudio</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Synchtank</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Reprtoir</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Harvest Media</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Cadenzabox</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Soundminer</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">MusicMaster</span></li>\n</ul>\n<h2><b></b></h2>\n<h4><b>Cyanite’s Key features:</b></h4>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Auto-Tagging</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Analyzes the </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2025/11/15/the-power-of-automatic-tagging-with-ai/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">full audio of each track and generates structured metadata</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> across 23 output categories, including genre and sub-genre, mood, character, movement, energy level, emotional dynamics, instrumentation, voice presence, BPM, key, meter, valence, arousal, musical era, and brand values, among others. Tags are applied consistently across the entire catalog, making legacy material and new releases equally searchable.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Similarity Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Finds tracks in your catalog that are </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/01/12/music-similarity-search-find-similar-songs/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">sonically similar to a reference</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">. References can be a track from your library, a YouTube link, or a Spotify preview. Library and YouTube tracks are analyzed in full. Similarity Search can be filtered by genre, key, BPM, and voice presence, and supports segment-based search so teams can match specific moments within a reference track rather than the full recording.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Free Text Search</b><span>: Allows teams to </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/03/02/how-to-prompt-using-cyanites-free-text-search/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">search for songs using natural language prompts</a></span><span>, including full sentences, sync briefs, scene descriptions, or creative directions in any language. The system interprets semantic meaning and matches intent to sound.</span><b></b><b></b></li>\n</ul>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Advanced Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: An </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2025/07/15/advanced-search-update/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">API-only add-on</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> that extends both Similarity and Free Text Search with:</span>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"2\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Multi-track similarity using up to 50 reference tracks simultaneously</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"2\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Similarity scores per track and segment</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"2\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Custom metadata filters</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"2\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Most relevant segment</span></li>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"2\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Up to 500 results per query</span></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li>&nbsp;<b>Auto-Descriptions</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Generates concise, neutral summaries of how each track sounds. These are derived entirely from audio analysis without relying on external language models, giving teams immediate context without having to listen first.</span><b></b></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Visualizations</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Displays analyzed metadata as </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/03/03/making-sense-of-music-data-data-visualizations/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">interactive graphs per track</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">, covering genre, mood, energy, instrument presence, and voice presence. Available in the web app and buildable via the API.</span></li>\n<li style=\"list-style-type: none;\">\n<ul></ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li aria-level=\"1\"><b>AI Music Detection</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">:</span> <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Analyzes audio and delivers a probability score estimating the likelihood of AI generation by systems such as Suno, Udio, or ElevenLabs. </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/ai-music-detection/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Available via a new REST API</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><b>Read more</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">:</span> <span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/01/13/from-upload-to-output-how-cyanite-turns-audio-into-reliable-metadata-at-scale/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">From upload to output: how Cyanite turns audio into reliable metadata at scale</span></a></span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_13  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>Cyanite is best suited for…</b></h4>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">A broad range of catalog-intensive organizations that need tagging and search connected in a single scalable workflow.&nbsp;</span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Music licensing platforms </b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">that need to tag large catalogs, enable faster brief responses, and surface relevant tracks from across their repertoire. For example, </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/01/20/how-melodie-music-combines-sound-based-ai-search-and-contextual-metadata-to-spotlight-original-australian-artists/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Melodie Music uses Cyanite</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> to combine sound-based search with contextual metadata, helping teams find suitable tracks quickly while prioritizing local artists.</span><b></b></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Music libraries </b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">that want to embed Similarity Search and Free Text Search directly into their own platforms via API.</span> <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">For instance, </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2024/04/13/ai-music-discovery-licensing/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Marmoset uses Cyanite</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> to power sound-based search within its platform, improving how tracks are indexed and discovered as new content is ingested.</span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Music tech platforms and streaming services</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> that need a sound-based recommendation and personalization layer without building their own AI infrastructure.</span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/04/08/how-thematic-uses-ai-powered-discovery-to-personalize-music-recommendations-at-scale/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"> <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Thematic, for example, uses Advanced Search</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> for personalized discovery, and you can read here how </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2026/03/24/pr-anghami-partners-with-cyanite-music-discovery-with-ai-powered-metadata-across-2-5-million-songs/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Anghami has partnered with Cyanite</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> to improve music recommendations at scale.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Distributors and record labels</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> that need consistent metadata applied to high-volume incoming tracks.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Audio branding agencies</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> that use mood, character, and brand value tags to align music with brand identity.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Teams without developer resources can start with the web app or a Tagging Export, while those building products or embedding search into their own platforms integrate via API.</span></p>\n<p><b>Learn more: </b><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://api-docs.cyanite.ai/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><b>Find all the API documentation here</b></a></span><b>.</b></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_14  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>Cyanite’s Limitations:</b></h4>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Advanced Search requires developer resources.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> Teams without technical capacity cannot access Advanced Search features through the web app.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Spotify references use 30-second previews only.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> Spotify only gives a 30-second snippet, so when you use a Spotify track as a similarity reference, only those 30 seconds are analyzed.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Video search is not available.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> We don’t currently support video-to-music matching as a search input.</span></span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Highly specific taxonomies still require human input.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> Context-driven tags such as film-specific categories, usage intent, or campaign labels cannot be generated reliably from audio alone and are better handled manually. We recommend a hybrid approach for these cases.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Spotify Audio Features are not replicated.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> We don’t offer equivalents of Spotify-style attributes such as danceability or acousticness. Our taxonomy is built around audible musical characteristics rather than behavioral or platform-derived signals.</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_15  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>Pricing model:</b></h4>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Our pricing is structured around catalog size rather than query volume, which is a meaningful distinction for teams evaluating long-term infrastructure costs.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Tagging is billed as a one-time fee per track. Search is billed as a recurring subscription per track. Both use volume-based tiers, meaning the cost per track decreases as catalog size grows. Search queries are unlimited for both Similarity Search and Free Text Search, so costs don’t increase with usage.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Auto-Tagging and search can also be purchased independently. For example, many teams start with search alone and add tagging later.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">This model provides cost predictability at scale, which helps teams evaluating infrastructure rather than a point solution. Usage-based pricing, where costs grow with query volume, can be harder to forecast as teams and catalogs expand.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">To get an estimate based on your catalog size, take a look at our </span><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.emlen.io/d/2mo7jK9pEe\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">pricing calculator</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_divider et_pb_divider_0 et_pb_divider_position_ et_pb_space\"><div class=\"et_pb_divider_internal\"></div></div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_16  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h3><strong>2. AIMS API</strong></h3>\n<p><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://www.aimsapi.com/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">AIMS</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> is an AI-powered music search platform built around audio-based technology.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Unlike systems that rely primarily on metadata, AIMS analyzes the audio signal directly to generate embeddings. These capture musical traits like rhythm, melody, and style. Search results are derived from those embeddings rather than from tags or text labels assigned to tracks.</span></p>\n<p><strong>AIMS is available in three ways:</strong><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<p><strong></strong></p>\n<ol>\n<li><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Via </span><b>API integration </b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">into proprietary platforms</span></span></span></li>\n<li><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">As a </span><b>plugin on existing catalog management platforms,</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> including Synchtank, DISCO, Cadenzabox, and Harvest Media</span></span></span></li>\n<li><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">As a </span><b>standalone web app </b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">for teams that want to get started without any development work</span></li>\n</ol></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_17  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>AIMS’ Key features:</b></h4>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Prompt Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Natural language search that accepts sync briefs, scene descriptions, mood references, and cultural or pop culture references. Searches cover the full catalog, including alternate versions and submixes.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Similarity Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Finds tracks based on audio similarity to a reference. Accepts YouTube, Spotify, Apple Music, and TikTok links, uploaded audio files, or tracks from within the catalog. Includes segment search, BPM prioritization, vocal ignore, and highlight markers that show where in a result the similarity is strongest.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Smart Projects and Dynamic Playlists</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Uses multiple reference tracks to establish a playlist theme and generates an ongoing stream of on-theme tracks that automatically updates as new music is added to the catalog.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Lyrics Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Finds music based on the meaning and sentiment of lyrics rather than exact word matches. For catalogs without existing lyric data, AIMS offers AudioShake’s transcription technology to generate lyrics automatically.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Video Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Matches music to a video clip that’s up to one minute long, analyzing visual tone, pace, and emotion.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li><b>Tagging</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Audio-based auto-tagging with support for custom tags, multiple languages, and export to DSPs and UGC platforms. Available as a standalone product or connected to the Similarity Search API via webhook.</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_18  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>AIMS API is best suited for…</b></h4>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">The standalone AIMS Platform is particularly well-suited to sync teams wanting immediate access to all search features without development resources. But, they need to be comfortable working within the AIMS interface.&nbsp;</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Teams that want to surface AIMS capabilities through their own platform or client-facing website will need developer capacity to integrate via API.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_19  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>AIMS’ Limitations:</b></h4>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Tagging is a secondary capability.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> AIMS’ core technology is audio similarity rather than tagging. The two operate as separate APIs that AIMS needs to connect on the backend.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>No similarity score is exposed via the API.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> Results are ranked by relevance but no numerical confidence value is returned, which limits developer control over how results are weighted or displayed in custom interfaces.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Teams integrating via API must build their own front-end interface from scratch.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> AIMS provides no UI component library for this purpose, though the demo application and code samples are available as reference.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Ranking behavior is fixed.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> Results are always sorted by descending similarity. It’s not possible to inject business logic, such as prioritizing new releases or boosting specific tracks in results.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>File format and size constraints apply to audio uploads. </b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Supported formats are MP3, WAV, and AIF(F), with a 120 MB file size limit. Segment search is capped at 60 seconds.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>AI music detection is not available. </b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">AIMS does not offer technology to flag AI-generated music in your catalog or new submissions.</span></li>\n</ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_20  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>Pricing model:</b></h4>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Not publicly available. Pricing is available upon request directly from AIMS.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_divider et_pb_divider_1 et_pb_divider_position_ et_pb_space\"><div class=\"et_pb_divider_internal\"></div></div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_21  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h3><strong>3. Harmix</strong></h3>\n<p><a href=\"https://www.harmix.ai/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Harmix</span></a><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> is an AI music search platform built for production music companies, TV and movie productions, record labels, and publishers. Its core focus is search: helping teams find the right track from their catalog using natural language, audio references, video input, or lyric-based queries. A standalone auto-tagging product is not offered.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Harmix is available in two ways:</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ol>\n<li><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Via </span><b>API integration</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> into proprietary platforms</span></li>\n<li><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">As a </span><b>web platform</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> that connects to your library without any coding required</span></li>\n</ol>\n<ul></ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_22  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>Key features:</b></h4>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Prompt Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Natural language search based on the sonic characteristics of the music you need. Accepts descriptions of instrumentation, mood, tempo, and context, as well as sync briefs and reference artists.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Similarity Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Finds tracks based on audio similarity to one or more reference tracks. Accepts audio links and uploaded files, with segment selection to narrow the reference to a specific part of a track.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Lyrics Story Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Finds music based on the thematic meaning and narrative of lyrics rather than sonic qualities. Accepts keywords or a description of the story or emotional arc the song should convey.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Video Search</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">: Analyzes a video or image input and finds music that matches the mood and visuals.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul></ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_23  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>Harmix is best suited for…</b></h4>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">The web platform suits internal sync teams and music supervisors who want immediate access to AI search without development resources.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Teams wanting to surface capabilities through their own platform or client-facing website will need developer capacity to integrate Harmix via API.</span></p>\n<ul></ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_24  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>Limitations:</b><b></b></h4>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<p><b></b></p>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>No auto-tagging or AI music detection product.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> Harmix doesn’t offer standalone tagging or AI music detection infrastructure. Teams that need to generate or enrich metadata at scale require a separate solution for that workflow.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>Metadata does not affect search results.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> According to the API documentation, metadata fields are used only for filtering, not for influencing search relevance.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>File format and size constraints apply to audio uploads.</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> Supported formats are MP3, WAV, and M4A, with a 120 MB file size limit.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"></span></p>\n<ul>\n<li style=\"font-weight: 400;\" aria-level=\"1\"><b>The “Highlights” feature requires manual activation</b><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">. It shows which segment of a result is most similar to the reference, but it’s not enabled by default. Teams need to contact Harmix support to activate.</span></li>\n</ul>\n<ul></ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_25  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h4><b>Pricing model:</b></h4>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Not publicly available but can be requested via a scheduled demo. Teams that want to test the product before committing can use a free trial. Harmix also runs a referral program for existing customers and partners, offering 100% of a referred client’s first-month fee as cash or account credit.</span></p>\n<ul></ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_divider et_pb_divider_2 et_pb_divider_position_ et_pb_space\"><div class=\"et_pb_divider_internal\"></div></div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_26  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h3><a href=\"https://music.ai/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><strong>4. Music.AI</strong></a></h3>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Music.AI is an audio intelligence platform covering stem separation, chord recognition, beat detection, and lyrics transcription. Cyanite integrates with Music.AI as a third-party addition, making AI tagging accessible directly within Music.AI workflows without a separate integration.</span></p>\n<ul></ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_27  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">AI infrastructure vs music CMS systems</span></h2>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">A common source of confusion when evaluating music AI tools is the difference between AI tagging and search infrastructure vs. music content management systems. They solve different problems, and understanding that distinction helps clarify which category you actually need.</span></p>\n<ul></ul></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_image et_pb_image_1\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.00@2x.png\" class=\"et_pb_lightbox_image\" title=\"\"><span class=\"et_pb_image_wrap \"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1584\" height=\"990\" src=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.00@2x.png\" alt=\"\" title=\"AI infrastructure vs music CMS systems\" srcset=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.00@2x.png 1584w, https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.00@2x-1280x800.png 1280w, https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.00@2x-980x613.png 980w, https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.00@2x-480x300.png 480w\" sizes=\"(min-width: 0px) and (max-width: 480px) 480px, (min-width: 481px) and (max-width: 980px) 980px, (min-width: 981px) and (max-width: 1280px) 1280px, (min-width: 1281px) 1584px, 100vw\" class=\"wp-image-20740\"></span></a>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_28  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Bridge.audio is a good example of why this distinction matters. It’s a music workflow platform that combines catalog management, AI-assisted tagging, sync discovery, and file sharing in one place. Cyanite is different, since it focuses exclusively on the analysis and search layer, going deeper on tagging, sound-based search, and API-level infrastructure for embedding discovery into other products.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Cyanite is not a CMS, nor is it designed to replace one. It’s directly integrated into and supports many of the major CMS platforms used across the industry.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">For teams using DISCO, for example, Cyanite’s tags can be exported as a CSV and re-imported into the platform. For most organizations, the two categories work in combination: the CMS handles operations, and AI like Cyanite handles the intelligence layer that makes the catalog searchable.</span></p>\n<p><b>Read more: </b><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/2024/07/30/music_content_management_systems_with_cyanite/\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"><b>Music CMS solutions compatible with Cyanite</b></a></span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">&nbsp;</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_29  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">So, which tool is right for you?</span></h2>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">The right system depends on what your core workflow actually requires.</span></p>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">If your catalog is hard to search, the issue is rarely search itself, but the metadata powering it. Search quality is downstream of metadata quality, so the first step is to identify where your discovery workflow actually breaks.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_image et_pb_image_2\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.41@2x.png\" class=\"et_pb_lightbox_image\" title=\"\"><span class=\"et_pb_image_wrap \"><img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"1576\" height=\"2477\" src=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.41@2x.png\" alt=\"\" title=\"What AI Music analysis tool is right for you?\" srcset=\"https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.41@2x.png 1576w, https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.41@2x-1280x2012.png 1280w, https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.41@2x-980x1540.png 980w, https://cyanite.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/CleanShot-2026-05-04-at-17.22.41@2x-480x754.png 480w\" sizes=\"(min-width: 0px) and (max-width: 480px) 480px, (min-width: 481px) and (max-width: 980px) 980px, (min-width: 981px) and (max-width: 1280px) 1280px, (min-width: 1281px) 1576px, 100vw\" class=\"wp-image-20738\"></span></a>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_30  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h3><strong>See what Cyanite can do for your catalog</strong></h3>\n<p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">The best way to evaluate Cyanite is to try it on your own music. Sign up for free, upload a set of tracks, and see how the tagging and search perform before making any commitment.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_button_module_wrapper et_pb_button_0_wrapper et_pb_button_alignment_center et_pb_module \">\n\t\t\t\t<a class=\"et_pb_button et_pb_button_0 et_pb_bg_layout_dark\" href=\"https://app.cyanite.ai/register\" target=\"_blank\" data-icon=\"5\">Try Cyanite for free</a>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_row et_pb_row_1\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_1  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_31  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_dark\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_text_inner\"><h2><span style=\"font-weight: 400; font-size: xx-large;\">FAQs</span></h2></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_module et_pb_code et_pb_code_0\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_code_inner\"><script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"FAQPage\",\n  \"mainEntity\": [\n    {\n      \"@type\": \"Question\",\n      \"name\": \"How long does the integration process take?\",\n      \"acceptedAnswer\": {\n        \"@type\": \"Answer\",\n        \"text\": \"Cyanite's API integration is typically completed on our side within just a few days. Cyanite is widely used across the industry, with clients like BMG and APM Music.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_1  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Q: Can AI understand a sync brief and find matching tracks?</h4>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">A: </span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Yes. Free text search systems can interpret natural language briefs, including scene descriptions and mood references, and match them to tracks in your catalog. Cyanite’s Free Text Search works in any language and understands semantic meaning rather than requiring exact keyword matches.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_2  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Q: How do I clean up inconsistent music tagging across a large catalog?</h4>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p><span style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><a href=\"https://cyanite.ai/music-tagging\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color: #cc99ff;\"><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Audio-based auto-tagging</span></a></span><span style=\"font-weight: 400;\"> applies the same analysis logic to every track regardless of when it was added or who tagged it. This is the most reliable way to bring consistency to legacy catalogs with uneven or incomplete metadata.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_3  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Q: Is there software that lets me search my music catalog with prompts?</h4>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>A: <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Yes. Cyanite, AIMS, and Harmix all support natural language search. Cyanite’s Free Text Search accepts full sentences, sync briefs, and scene descriptions in any language.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_4  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Q: What’s the best AI for analyzing music mood and genre?</h4>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>A: <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Cyanite’s taxonomy covers 13 simple moods, 131 advanced moods, 23 main genres, 58 sub-genres, and over 5,000 free genre tags, all generated from audio analysis rather than user behavior or existing metadata.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div><div class=\"et_pb_toggle et_pb_module et_pb_accordion_item et_pb_accordion_item_5  et_pb_toggle_close\">\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"et_pb_toggle_title\">Q: What’s the most accurate software for finding similar songs in a music library?</h4>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"et_pb_toggle_content clearfix\"><p>A: <span style=\"font-weight: 400;\">Accuracy depends on how deeply a system analyzes audio. Cyanite compares tracks across multiple acoustic dimensions simultaneously. It also supports segment-based search and multi-track references, filtering for genre, key, BPM, and voice presence to refine results further.</span></p></div>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t</div>\t\t</div>\n\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\t<div
